# Current Formal Frontier Toward Proving or Disproving the Riemann Hypothesis

## Research map of HTGeometry, HilbertMetric, and RHTower

**Corpus cutoff:** 11 August 2026, committed snapshot
**694f9bfda4ce645951b9a0c4e435cb8f9e58c357**.

Formal status means theorem declarations reachable from the committed HTGeometry, HilbertMetric,
or RHTower umbrellas at this snapshot. A proof present only in a paper card is marked
paper-proved or provisional. A Lean file not reached by a committed umbrella is not counted as
formal frontier. The map records present state, not research chronology.

## Abstract

This paper maps every proof architecture toward the Riemann hypothesis currently instantiated by
the corpus. Routes are quotiented by their first unresolved source theorem, not by shared
vocabulary or final RH criterion. Each dossier states endpoint, proved mechanism, obstruction,
open core, and retired subclasses. Lean declarations are authoritative; paper claims are
separated from formal ones.

Neither RH nor its negation is proved. The independent live routes are C1, D2, E, G, L, O, P, Q,
R, T, and U. The unlettered CCM-FU lane is also live. Route N is terminalized: its final
zero-fracture premise is RH-equivalent. Routes T and U are new independent
architectures exposed by the enlarged corpus:

- **Route T** converts the mass-shifted Xi divisor geometry into one exact critical outgoing
  current and then into a finite centered von Mangoldt polynomial inside a moving mass light cone.
- **Route U** follows the first loss of positivity in the shifted-Xi Hankel tower and identifies
  its crossing form with a completed Euler--Gamma extension of an adaptive null flag.

These are not renamed versions of existing routes. Route T has a first-order critical
von-Mangoldt flux endpoint, whereas E has squarefree Möbius parity transport and terminalized N
has a prime-normal domain classifier. Route U has an adaptive finite Hankel null-face crossing endpoint,
whereas G seeks a cofinal Suzuki two-angle meromorphic carrier and P seeks one compact positive
moment measure.

No twelfth live lettered route is instantiated at this cutoff. Four possible Route-V
architectures now have nontrivial cores: conservative Euler-shell/Witt scale completion,
triptic orbital-divisor normal form, tropical-projective source-normal descent, and relative
formation-completion current. The first
lacks its literal jointly convergent source inverse pair; the second now has an exact additive and
multiplicative kernel theorem but no coefficient-faithful Xi landing or critical reflection law;
the third has a prime-clock projective-width selector but no Xi-typed observer/descent theorem.
The fourth has an umbrella-reachable centered-jet/evenization classification, but no literal
arithmetic relative extension or source law annihilating its hidden current.
The reciprocal-character virial and inverse-response Ward laws give a concrete possible producer
inside the existing C1/CCM/Gauss architecture, not a fourth independent candidate route.
The newly formal adelic Plucker-history, interaction-spectrum formation wall, essential-formation
quotient, passive-cycle law, and static-heterogeneity observer also do not instantiate Route V.
They give exact source mechanics, including the prime reflection wall at exponent one and
invariance under summable compensators, but no theorem identifies physical off-center Xi
displacement with their formation parameter or lands their neutral selector in a fixed Xi fibre.

## 1. Reading rules and status vocabulary

**Endpoint** is a proved implication or equivalence whose conclusion is RH. It is a receiver, not
evidence for its premise.

**Open core** is first unproved source statement after all proved connectors are removed. An
assumption equivalent to RH is not called progress.

**Closed mechanism** means corpus proves advertised construction cannot provide required
information. Closure is scoped: it does not refute every future mechanism using similar language.

**Positive survivor** is strongest nontrivial theorem exposed by a failed route: rigidity,
classification, exact boundary, or replacement design constraint.

**Independent route** requires a literal source object, an unconditional chain to an RH endpoint,
and a first unresolved producer not identical under the corpus's source-isogeny comparisons to an
existing route.

## 3. Common source architecture and merger verdict

The corpus constructs a source atlas far beyond classical scalar criteria:

1. Wold activation gives zeta ratios and logarithmic derivatives.
2. Squarefree breadth and prime-power depth are separated before scalarization.
3. Möbius depth differences, Fisher damping, primitive von Mangoldt promotion, and Gamma
   whitening are explicit.
4. Regular and singular source-to-Xi landings retain divisor and multiplicity jets.
5. Reflection is tracked before positive energy, determinant, or scalar frequency fusion.

Several apparent routes share one horizontal obstruction. Route R's graded determinant anomaly,
Route L's monodromy/Green obstruction, and the CCM Bockstein are source-isogenous realizations of
one reflection-odd horizontal class. Increasing vector dimension, Kummer depth, or route count
does not raise this atlas rank. The common operation previously missing was:

~~~
SOURCE-ZERO-TO-HORIZONTAL-CARRIER:
construct a source-derived relation which retains and orients the horizontal class before
bounded scalarization, while preserving prime labels, divisor multiplicity, and both reflection
orientations.
~~~

`SourceZeroHorizontalCarrier.lean` closes this operation without bounded scalarization. For every
Xi-divisor multiplicity copy it pairs complete-resolution reflection displacement with the full
ordinary-prime Gamma Bockstein family. Ordered adjacent source ratios recover the exact horizontal
grade; both components reverse under `rho ↦ 1-conj rho`; source-tail log times every prime
component is strictly positive exactly off the critical line. The physical Rees--corona landing
retains prime, depth, divisor, and multiplicity provenance.

This completes carrier construction and orientation, not annihilation. Lean proves global carrier
vanishing equivalent to RH. The pairing of the two retained cofinal orientations is now itself
constructed and signed: weighting the product of the tail-log and prime-Bockstein odd components
by the assembled Gauss residue current gives the Hodge mass current
`H(q,p) = (log 2/2)·current(p)·density(p)·t_q·(p^{t_q} - p^{-t_q})`, `t_q = 2 Re q - 1` —
unconditionally nonnegative, reflection-even, label-faithful, with exact critical radical
(`GaussHodgeMassCurrent.lean`). The Hodge sign of the constructed pairing therefore holds; zero
total mass of this nonnegative current is proved equivalent to RH. A formalized interface
firewall shows the mass-zero law cannot follow from the pairing's own interface even with
divisor-reflection closure: an explicit reflection-closed off-line configuration has positive
mass. The seam-energy bridge (`GaussHodgeSeamEnergyBridge.lean`, with the hyperbolic
comparison layer in `HTGeometry/HyperbolicBounds.lean`) then proves the Hodge mass current and
the Burnol FE--Schwarz seam reflection energy are one invariant: two-sided domination with
explicit constants (`log p/2` and `2√p/log p`; every divisor copy is proved to lie in the open
strip), pointwise co-vanishing, and RH ⟺ prime-2 seam-charge vanishing ⟺ prime-2 Burnol
character unimodularity ⟺ full seam-energy vanishing on the divisor. Because the corpus's seam
layer already evaluates the full prime sum — seam energy at a copy equals the convexity second
difference `M(1+2σ)+M(3−2σ)−2M(2)` of the moment curve `M(β)=Σ_p (log p)² p^{−β}` — the
common open core is now exactly one **horizontal divisor transform**: the divisor average of
that convexity defect. A formalized information-gap theorem fences it: the FE-pair sample of
the prime character is the constant `1` and no function of it computes the Hermitian sample
`‖char‖` — the missing evaluation lies strictly outside the holomorphic sample algebra
(the `Σg²` vs `Σ|g|²` gap as a theorem). Known fencing no-gos: density envelopes cannot decide
the horizontal transform, and its bilinear upgrade lands on diagonal-vs-off-diagonal
separation — the MR/correlation wall. External anchors: Suzuki's screw function
(arXiv:2206.03682) and Connes--Consani Sonin-space Weil positivity (arXiv:2006.13771).
`GaussHodgeCoherenceBudget.lean` then prices the residue exactly: the divisor is
conjugation-closed and `char(ρ)·char(conj ρ) = p^{2Reρ−1}` — the missing transform is the
conjugate-pair diagonal of a squared explicit formula (the pair-correlation classification as
a theorem) — and a Cauchy--Schwarz coherence budget makes the Hodge/seam mass an exact
certificate for zero-side exponential-sum coherence, collapsing to square-root cancellation
`2·|F|` under RH. The successor gate is a limit-exchange statement: vertical time-averaging
extracts the (multiplicity-squared) diagonal for finite families via the completed-time torus
layer; what is open is the interchange of that mean with the divisor closure, priced by
multiplicity squares (zero-simplicity layer) and fenced by the truncate-first co-Poisson
no-go. That interchange is now itself PROVED (`GaussHodgeVerticalExtraction.lean`): for every
ℓ¹ weight and every unimodular mode family — no gap, separation, or correlation input — the
Cesàro mean-square converges to the equal-mode pair diagonal (dominated convergence over
coefficient pairs), instantiated over the full divisor with the vertical flow at the
ordinates; the reflected partner shares the ordinate and the reflection-odd 2×2 block of the
extracted diagonal is exactly the cosh defect. The single unproven step left in the whole
chain is sourcing the ℓ¹ vertical pencil `Σ_ρ v_ρ·char(ρ)·e^{i n Im ρ}` from prime-side data:
the vertical mode is a conjugate-pair (bilinear) sample, and the information-gap theorem
proves no holomorphic channel supplies it. `GaussHodgePencilSourcing.lean` then closes the
loop on that step: the vertical mode has a holomorphic FE companion `e^{ρ−1/2}` whose pencil
IS prime-side data (`char_p·(e^{ρ−1/2})ⁿ = char_{p·eⁿ}` — the zero current at flowed scales),
whose polar factorization exhibits the sourcing defect as the radial mass factor `e^{t/2}`,
and **sourcing ⟺ RH** exactly (`riemannHypothesis_iff_feMode_eq_verticalMode`); under RH
every vertical-pencil value collapses to explicit-formula data. The Gauss/Hodge/seam
programme is now a single closed equivalence loop with every link an unconditional Lean
theorem; its one non-circular entry point is an independent (necessarily bilinear) evaluation
of the vertical pencil. `GaussHodgeWeilPolarizationFusion.lean` fuses the loop with the
corpus's Weil-positivity interface: at the prime exponential test the Weil pair summand of
`xiWeilPolarization` is constantly 1 (blindness, in-interface), the pair-square law holds for
every test over each reflection pair, the sharp-difference square of the prime test is the
cosh defect, hence the H-mass density is exactly the per-pair gap between the Hermitian pair
energy and the Weil polarization value, RH ⟺ that gap closes at prime 2, and the eigen-Gram
of the polarization is unconditionally supported on conjugate pairs with value
multiplicity·XiC′(α)·conj XiC′(conj α). A Gate-II entry — compressed-trace positivity matched
to `xiWeilPolarization` — now lands directly on the H-mass. The remaining content of this entry
is a source-derived global compressed-trace/intersection identity whose positivity controls the
Hermitian pair energy, not merely the blind Weil pair sample.
`GaussHodgeWeilGNSSocket.lean` makes the handoff exact: RH is equivalent to a global coordinate
square realization of every square-summable diagonal Weil value, and such a realization is
equivalent to Hodge-mass vanishing. The converse realization constructed under RH is tautological,
so this is a receiver socket, not a square-realization theorem. The missing producer is an
unconditional source-derived square/GNS or compressed-trace realization on the actual multi-place
class-space object.
`GaussHodgeWeilKreinDecomposition.lean` executes the direct reflection-pair construction and
classifies its exact obstruction. Unconditionally
`4·Re W(F,F) = Σ‖pair sum‖² − Σ‖pair difference‖²` on square-summable tests. The negative
sector's prime-test density is exactly the Hodge/cosh mass, and its annihilation for every test,
the square realization, Hodge-mass vanishing, and RH are equivalent. Thus indefinite Krein
realization is unconditional but not a producer: the direct pair-sum GNS subroute leaves precisely
the RH-bearing negative sector. A Gate-II theorem must annihilate that sector through an
independent arithmetic class-space law, not repackage it.
`GaussHodgeWeilFundamentalSymmetry.lean` names that sector's geometry: the parity flip
`J F = F ∘ conj` is an unconditional fundamental symmetry of the Weil form — J-self-adjoint,
involutive, with J-twisted form equal to the divisor ℓ² inner product — so the GNS Hilbert space
a realization would build already exists as the J-metric ℓ². Divisor parity is W-orthogonal;
the even sector carries an unconditional square realization, the odd sector is exactly minus ℓ²;
the diagonal Weil form is real. RH is the degeneracy law: RH ⟺ every divisor-odd test vanishes
on the divisor ⟺ W equals its own J-twist. The producer target is thereby minimal: any exogenous
positivity that forces J-twist invariance of W closes RH; nothing weaker than killing the odd
ℓ² sector suffices.
`GaussHodgeWeilEntireDetection.lean` minimizes the producer's burden: a multiplicity-exact
entire witness (normalized terminal divided quotients at an off-line point and its reflection)
shows the rough test class adds nothing — RH ⟺ Weil positivity on entire square-summable tests
alone, ⟺ entire divisor-odd tests vanish on the divisor, ⟺ parity-twist invariance on entire
tests. Any producer controlling the analytic class (explicit formula, compressed trace, CC
compression) therefore controls everything; no positivity on non-analytic tests is ever needed.
`GaussHodgeWeilDeviationMoment.lean` and `GaussHodgeWeilSymmetryInsufficiency.lean` close the
producer question from both sides. The deviation moment identity factors the negative sector
exactly: Re W(F,F) = Σ‖even‖² − Σ (β−1/2)²·‖ΔF‖² (deviation × divided difference), with the
unconditional strip bound Re W ≥ Σ‖even‖² − (1/4)Σ‖ΔF‖², and RH ⟺ the deviation moment
vanishes. The insufficiency theorem proves the source cannot be symmetry: an explicit even,
conjugation-real entire comparison function with strip-placed off-line zeros carries an entire
test of negative Weil diagonal, so any producer must consume the Euler product. The open core
in final form: an arithmetic inequality dominating the deviation-weighted divided-difference
moment by the even energy on entire tests.
`GaussHodgeWeilArithmeticDeviationGap.lean` makes the Euler product act on the Weil form for

and by the de la Vallée-Poussin log⁹ margin above — improving the deviation-moment lower bound

positivity repels zeros from the strip walls (weight < 1/4); the socket needs the weight at 0.
That remaining distance is the lane's exact open problem.
`GaussHodgeWeilTotalDeviationMass.lean` compresses the wall to one number: the total
normalized deviation mass Σ'(Im α_q)²/‖α_q‖² converges unconditionally (genus-one Hadamard +
strip), and RH ⟺ it equals zero. The all-heights arithmetic ceiling (uniform gap below height
3, log⁹ margin above) bounds every squared deviation strictly below 1/4 and yields the
unrestricted weighted deviation-moment bound for the Weil form and the ceiling-weighted cap on
the mass. The lane's entire content is now: known mathematics traps the mass in [0, ceiling·Hadamard];
RH is the statement that it sits at the left endpoint. This theorem family is committed and
umbrella-reachable; it sharpens the endpoint but does not supply the arithmetic law forcing the
mass to vanish.
`GaussPositiveCubicCriticalMass.lean` closes a tempting trace substitute negatively. The literal
folded Gauss block is a positive operator; positive-moment log-convexity amplifies its retained
first Fourier coefficient into the cubic diagonal. For three components, both reflected
orientations eventually dominate a fixed fraction of the critical sampled energy, every cubic
diagonal term is nonnegative, and the partial sums diverge to `+infinity`. Thus an unrenormalized
positive cubic trace cannot be the missing finite annihilator. Any global Gauss trace law must be
signed, relatively renormalized, or cancel through a distinct class-space differential.
`GaussReciprocalCharacterAdmission.lean` supplies a second exact normalization: the Hodge mass is
the virial derivative of one reciprocal-character split energy, has a quadratic centered-rate
lower bound, and its all-prime family is summable exactly at centered rate zero. Universal
inverse-response Ward mechanics shows what could force such a positive virial current to vanish:
a common inverse formation whose mixed source acceleration is balanced by its boundary anomaly.
The strict invariant-trace specialization is grade-blind, so any viable Gauss producer must be an
anomalous/source-relative Ward identity, not an ordinary trace. No theorem constructs this
arithmetic inverse formation or proves the required compressed-trace admission; the summability
condition itself is exactly RH. This is a sharpened C1/CCM/Gauss entry mechanism, not Route V.
Route L asks for the common horizontal annihilator through the resolved-Green
rational-Witt/Milnor source annihilator; Route R asks for it through singular Cartier/Stokes
non-amplification. These target realizations remain distinct even though they retain the same
horizontal class.

The new longitudinal--transverse conservation law explains why this common atlas does not itself
select. Normalized source resolution lives in the zero-codiagonal radical; every intervention that
preserves that radical leaves the divisor coordinate unchanged except for a predeclared scalar
action. A merged architecture therefore needs an independently source-generated nonzero mixing
defect before physical landing. Nonzero mixing is only necessary: it must still preserve labels,
multiplicity, reflection, and the route-specific admission law.

The residue-promotion anomaly makes this constraint sharper. Attaching a new law cell preserves
the existing residue exactly iff the attachment already annihilates the defect. Any compensator
has rank at least the anomaly rank; a fitted rank-minimal compensator splits off as a contractible
summand and creates no new law. Coordinate-stable compensators are infinite-dimensional, no
holomorphic compensator can reproduce the physical mixed defect before divisor specialization,
and the pre-reflection charge has separation rank exactly two. Thus a viable R/L/CCM comparison
must be genuinely mixed and source-relative before specialization; finite rank-one or holomorphic
repairs only re-present or erase the obstruction.

This does not merge C1, D2, E, G, L, O, P, Q, R, T, and U into one serial super-route. Route N is
terminalized and contributes a receiver/diagnostic invariant, not another producer. The live routes
have different admission and producer theorems. R/L/CCM comparisons cannot be counted as independent
evidence merely because they use different target geometries.

The remaining problems are therefore not mostly missing connector lemmas. They are source
selection, source-relative coercivity, singular boundary annihilation/positive closure, or
genuinely oscillatory all-prime cancellation before information-losing completion.

Every resolvent moment is unconditionally positive once `X>=(n+1)^2`; every Hankel form with

Chebyshev window retaining at least `1-C^(-2)` of mass, the corrected column-load formula

P_(sigma,N) nu_(sigma,N)(T) W_(sigma,x)(T) = exp(-x m(T)).
~~~

Thus normalized source-faithful observables and relation flows are Euler-gauge invariant. The

Separately, for fixed observables under the bare Euler Bernoulli product laws below aperture `N`,
the exact finite likelihood product and local defect comparison give

~~~
chiSq(nu_(1+delta,N) || nu_(1,N))
  <= exp(primeClockCutoffEnergy N (delta/2)) - 1.
~~~

Therefore every eventually nonnegative `delta_N` with `delta_N log N -> 0` has chi-square distance
to the critical bare source tending to zero, and exact Cauchy--Schwarz transfers fixed finite
observables with controlled critical second moment. The stronger formal rate
`delta_N log N sqrt(log N) -> 0` gives `chi-square * log N -> 0`, and the formal normalized-ratio
theorem then gives qualitative `o(1)` transfer on a critical unit shell of mass `>> 1/log N`.
For the forced tilt this generic normalized error is only
`O(log log N / sqrt(log N))`, far above `N^(-1/2+o(1))`. Hence chi-square supplies no RH-scale
endpoint transport. It remains useful for coarse fixed-observable comparisons, but supplies no
incidence, incoming-capacity, seam, or recurrence estimate. Literal physical relations use the
exact gauge cancellation above instead.

Current HTGeometry incidence and prime-port files do not close the remaining column estimate.
`PauliXorIncidenceHypercontractivityLaw.lean` proves a dimension-free fourth-incidence bound
`I_4(c) <= 9^(4q) ||c||_2^4` for nonnegative Boolean/Pauli coefficients of Hamming weight at most
`q`. At Route E breadth `q=O(log log X)` its constant is only `X^(o(1))`, but it controls **raw**
collision energy. For total physical mass `asymp X`, this reaches only `X^(1+o(1))`; the Hellinger
consumer needs centered weighted column excess `X^(o(1))`. Low degree alone cannot improve this:
a source-dependent increment of the same low breadth can send every row to one prescribed column.
Thus this theorem becomes useful only after an independent centered/factorized mixing law for the
actual arithmetic kernel, which is precisely absent. `PrimePhasePortRecurrence.lean` makes

Paper-level recombination now corrects the interpretation of this final term. For

the square-multiple polynomial is identically zero, and

The `n=m` part is positive and at most `2WM`; if `(n,m)=1`, the coefficient is exactly one.

The same recombination exposes a new positive avenue. The unrestricted coprime finite-prime

T_Q(t) = product_{p in Q} (1-p^(it)-p^(-it)).

Its pointwise maximum `3^|Q|` is approached cofinally exactly at simultaneous antiscale recurrence,

integral_{-W}^W |T_Q(t)|^2 dt
 <= (2W + 4 R_Q^2 (1+|Q| log 3)) 3^|Q|.

For reciprocal marker weights `1,1/p,1/p`, the normalized cube has finite-height mean square

(2W + 4 R_Q^2(1+|Q| log 3))

The product tends to zero with harmonic marker mass, although its pointwise modulus returns

~~~

The product is bounded by `exp(-(1/2) sum_(p in Q)p^(-r))`, so it vanishes exactly throughout

**Blunt verdict.** Old statement “define R_X” is obsolete. Missing theorem is rate-sharp weighted
physical projection/all-reporter expansion plus recurrence/seam orientation, direct
recurrence-weighted inverse-load/column-load Dirichlet energy, or product-fibre conditional parity
energy for the actual prime-exponent histogram regime with aperture admission and seam descent.
In the third lane, exact target is now control of the displayed histogram-bias product (or its
balanced-incidence exponent) plus explicit Gibbs curvature, not an unnamed conditional energy.
Finite component-orbit counting, product-history Gibbs distortion, and Gibbs-to-uniform comparison
are formally closed on the third lane. All three live alternatives are now fixed arithmetic
quotient problems. Unquantified unit capacity, replica cardinality, signed flux decomposition, and

collapse source. Any order-one pre-Gamma packet-diagonal successor is a new route.

**Paper-closed singular successor.** Complete Möbius prime-normal packets now carry an exact

then for every honest depth `m>=3`,

~~~
(r/p)^(m/2) U_(m log(r/p)) (R_(p,m)-mu(p) Theta) = R_(r,m).
~~~

For `2 -> 3`, its input converges strongly to zero while its output converges to `-Theta`; its

the leading nonzero Xi multiplicity jet. This closes construction of a singular pre-divisor

normalizer by `(r/p)^(m(2 Re(s)-1))`, so reflected compatibility is exactly the critical-line

absorbs this explicit anomaly while preserving the fixed physical coefficient. The result is
dependency-closed on paper and not yet formalized in Lean.

**Paper-closed full-grade Steinberg normal transgression.** The counterterm's source coefficient
is no longer unspecified.  At every depth `m`, the complementary rational pair

~~~
x_m = 3^m/(2^m+3^m),    y_m = 2^m/(2^m+3^m)
~~~

is an honest Steinberg pair and has ratio `x_m/y_m=(3/2)^m`.  In the rational Kummer group
algebra, let `P_m=([x_m]-1)([y_m]-1)`, let `N[u]=(log u)[u]`, and put

~~~
q_m(T)=T(T-L_m)(2T-L_m)/(a_m b_m (a_m-b_m)),
a_m=log x_m, b_m=log y_m, L_m=a_m+b_m.
~~~

The exact coefficient-only identity is

~~~
[y_m]^-1 q_m(N) P_m = [(3/2)^m]-1.
~~~

Its horizontal character is `(3/2)^(m(2 Re(s)-1))-1`, exactly the full-depth successor
reflection anomaly.  Under the half-density Deninger representation it is
`T_(2->3,m)-I`; on the complete tail `h_m=R_(2,m)+Theta` it satisfies

~~~
(T_(2->3,m)-I) h_m = R_(3,m)-h_m -> -Theta.
~~~

Thus algebraic normal escape, physical reflection anomaly, and the nonclosable analytic
successor edge are one source object.  This is not an ordinary Milnor endomorphism or Hochschild
boundary: the Kummer regulator of the output is `m log(3/2) != 0`, while it kills the entire
Steinberg ideal.  General tangent-K/additive-K background is literature-known; the
depth-complementary selector and its exact identification with the complete Deninger--Möbius
successor are project-specific. This closes the successor-coordinate normal but not the actual
Gamma clock; the direct Gamma-six construction below supersedes it on the critical path. An
artificial cone declaring either class a boundary, or ordinary cyclic descent, is forbidden.
Full proof:
`../2-fully-proven/new_scientific_knowledge/deninger/deninger-steinberg-kummer-successor-normal-transgression.md`.

**Paper-closed direct Gamma-six normal transgression.** The successor clock mismatch is now
removed rather than repaired by a fitted scalar.  The finite Möbius correction

~~~
H_(6,m)=R_(1,m)-Theta
  +sum_(p in {2,3,5}) p^(-m/2) U_(-m log p) Theta
  =sum_(n>=6) mu(n)n^(-m/2)U_(-m log n)Theta
~~~

satisfies, for every `m>=3`,

~~~
6^(m/2) U_(m log 6) H_(6,m)=R_(6,m),
H_(6,m)->0,   R_(6,m)->Theta != 0.
~~~

The honest Steinberg pair

~~~
x_m=6^m/(1+6^m),   y_m=1/(1+6^m)
~~~

has normal extraction `[6^m]-1`.  Reversing the normal orientation swaps `x_m,y_m` in the
same unoriented cell and extracts `[6^(-m)]-1`, so the oriented source boundary is

~~~
[6^m]-[6^(-m)].
~~~

Its horizontal character is exactly
`6^(m(2 Re(s)-1))-6^(-m(2 Re(s)-1))=K_(6^m)(s)`.  At common `(2,3)` Gamma
depth `m-1`, `6^m` is the literal modular clock; at `m=1` this is the fixed actual
Gamma/Burnol/BV coefficient up to its already fixed positive calibration.  Both orientations
have explicit Tate--Wold primitives

~~~
[6^m]-1=(1-[6^(-1)]) sum_(j=1)^m [6^j],
[6^(-m)]-1=(1-[6]) sum_(j=1)^m [6^(-j)].
~~~

The oriented Kummer regulator is `2m log 6 != 0`. Hence ordinary cyclic descent cannot close
the class. These two primitives are algebraic, not two singular transports of one vanishing
physical source. On the complete corrected tail,

~~~
(T_(6,m)-T_(6,m)^(-1)) H_(6,m)
  = R_(6,m)-T_(6,m)^(-2)R_(6,m) -> Theta,

while any reciprocal landing `T_(6,m)^(-1)K_m -> Y != 0` forces

exponent the six-tail multiplier

~~~
B_6(s)=zeta(s)^(-1)-1+2^(-s)+3^(-s)+5^(-s)
~~~

satisfies `Xi(s)B_6(s)=A(s)+Xi(s)P_6(s)`, where

The functional-equation mate

~~~
-A(s)/A(1-s) B_6(1-s)
~~~

is a canonical opposite pole germ, but scalar pole cancellation is reciprocity data and is

fixed-reader residue-cancellation law. Full proofs:

are Vandermonde-complete at matching aperture, but the infinite weak-star orbit has no derivative
in unweighted `ell-infinity`; its generator exists only on logarithmically weighted preduals.
Recurrence, finite tomography, and positive norm therefore do not select a faithful global
completion or an infinitesimal source law. This is a compatibility/no-go theorem, not Route V.

The constitutive universal-law-field modules add exact source mechanics, not an RH route.
`ConstitutiveInteractionSpectrumMechanics.lean` identifies finite interaction work, truncation
tail, and coherence loss, and proves the literal prime formation is summable exactly above
exponent one; reflection across one preserves formation phase only at the fixed point.
`ConstitutiveEssentialFormationMechanics.lean` proves finite or summable compensators cannot move
that wall. `ConstitutivePassiveCycleMechanics.lean` proves every defect vanishes on a closed cycle
whose legs are passive in one work norm. `ConstitutiveStaticHeterogeneityObserver.lean` identifies
posterior variance as the exact dissipation of mean rate and classifies zero variance as active-rate
collapse. These laws could constrain a future source selector, but no declaration types Xi
divisors, completed Euler--Gamma formation, or a physical landing. They therefore neither advance
an existing Tier-1 source theorem nor instantiate Route V.

None of these theorems constrains the landed divisor to
the critical line or supplies an RH endpoint. Route S remains research infrastructure, not yet an
RH proof route.

### Route T — mass-shifted critical outgoing current [OPEN — NEW]

**Endpoint.** For every legal a>1/2, sigma>0, and 1/2<d<a, Lean proves

~~~
RH
 iff uniform boundedness in u>=0 of
    sum_(2<=n<=floor(exp(d*u/(d-1/2))))
      (vonMangoldt(n)-1) n^(-1/2) F_u(log n),
~~~

equivalently uniform boundedness of exact mass-test pairing with finite centered Dirichlet
polynomial.

**Proved mechanism.**

1. Mass-shifted Xi spectral trace converts transverse zero width into vertical growth.
2. Completed Chebyshev error flux admits exact Volterra-adjoint identity; polar and explicit faces
   are uniformly bounded.
3. Critical cofinal atoms equal legal bottom Euler source in limit.
4. Continuous interpolation and floor sawtooth are uniformly bounded.
5. Conjugation reduces to u>=0.
6. Superluminal logarithmic-time tail is uniformly bounded.
7. Complete-cell Abel summation reduces cone to centered atoms; partial cell and terminal
   remainder are bounded.

9. Integer face, true-prime reduction, prime-square tail, and cosine form are formalized in

10. Exact phase classification: `log n<u` gives one timelike saddle
    `x_*=-a log(n)/sqrt(u^2-log(n)^2)` with phase `a sqrt(u^2-log(n)^2)`; `log n>=u` gives no
    stationary point. Route N reciprocal escape embeds into this same unit-proper-time boost shell.

    damping quadratic. In `0<u<h`, this rate strictly exceeds both `h-u` and
    `sqrt(h^2-u^2)`. The induced critical balance slope
    `a/sqrt(a^2-1/4)` is strictly smaller than the limiting horizontal-strip slope

12. Exact branch-free transform: `x=a*sinh(t)` converts the mass Fourier transform into a

    Under full-ground nonnegativity, `dim ker>=1+2E`; negative roots with positive terminal mass are
    impossible, and a zero root creates odd degeneracy. Compact resolvent permits only finitely
    many multiple roots. If the ground is simple, every root is simple, every terminal scalar is
    positive, and every squared root is strictly positive. An explicit radical pencil moves one copy
    of any repeated root to an
    arbitrary real location; its polar-dark member moves it to `-1/4`. Thus polar evaluation is
    not cyclic on the degenerate ground and cannot restore simplicity. This is paper-proved in
    `mass-shifted-hard-edge-root-jet-radical-rigidity` and
    `mass-shifted-hard-edge-parity-jet-inertia`. The former abstract
    `HTGeometry/NullRootDivisionSpectralLaw.lean` wrapper was removed from the working tree before
    acceptance: it assumed the radical/positivity data and did not construct the literal
    completed-Xi hard edge, complete `m-1` flag, terminal mass, or ground-multiplicity conclusion.
    No literal Route-U Lean result is credited from it.
51. Frozen/current comparison is now exact at literal arithmetic-source level. If `Q_w(A)` is

    path-independent cocycle, not genuine shape holonomy. For current canonical admission

    `Q_w(P_(n,a))=2 h_n(w) E_n(w)+T_w(P_(n,w),P_(n,a))`; negativity is equivalent to

54. The paper-proved hard-edge parity-jet inertia law resolves the formerly unexplained excess
    directions. For the squared-variable hard-edge measure, multiplication by Fourier variable

55. The finite ancestor of negative hard-edge root signature is now exact. If `x_i` is a selected
    flat-face quadrature node at mass `y`, then its hard coordinate is
    `u_i=x_i^(-1)-y`, not `x_i`. The finite polynomial form
    `E_y(q)=L_y((1-yX)q^2)` has inertia equal to the counts of nodes below, on, and above the

    After the actual far-right normalization this is exactly the first Hausdorff-difference matrix

56. Individual selected Gauss nodes now have an exact source evolution. In every positive

    `u_i'=2n h_n/(x_i^2 gamma_i)>0`. At a flat face whose first nonzero Padé defect is

57. The surviving hard Padé residue now has a literal restricted-Weil source meaning.  If a

58. The canonical Padé resolvent has an exact moving-center worldline.  At fixed absolute mass

59. The simple-ground Fourier real-zero theorem is parity-free.  Connes--van Suijlekom prove the

    `T'=D^2-|D^2 xi><D eta|` on the odd sector.  The commutator makes `T'` self-adjoint in the

    This is paper-proved in `mass-shifted-pade-carathéodory-boundary-resolution-law`; novelty is
    unverified and no Lean declaration is claimed.
60a. Finite-head resolvent depletion cannot sharpen item 60 by a matched-jet or Carathéodory
    estimate.  Moving the positive-real boundary from `-c` to `-c_y`, even with
    `c_y asymp sqrt(y) log y`, moves a bounded forbidden atom to a resolvable disk depth.  But every
    removed squared-Xi divisor point reappears as an opposite rational pole.  For bounded pole

    invisible to those jets.  This is paper-proved in
    `mass-shifted-pade-carathéodory-boundary-resolution-law`; no Lean declaration is claimed.
60b. The Connes--van Suijlekom rank-two commutator forces an exact parity-multiplicity balance:

    simple.  If a positive terminal mass is admitted to the full odd form, its negative odd floor
    is therefore a unique simple eigenvalue, and item 59 makes that lower-ground Fourier transform

61. The fixed-shift resolvent-Hankel endpoint is now exact Lean. For every `X>=0`, positivity of
    every finite resolvent Hankel form is equivalent to RH. The order-two Turán defect is bounded

    `M(X)<=2H/(X+1)^2` and `M(X)=0` is equivalent to RH. Thus the full obstruction is one decaying

62. The unconditional sector is unbounded in degree. Every moment of order `n` has positive real
    part for `X>=(n+1)^2`; every Hankel form with nonnegative coefficients is positive for
    `X>=(2N+1)^2`. Any negative witness there must change coefficient sign. Equivalently, the
    squared Xi radial field has alternating derivative signs for all orders on the quadratic far
    ray (and through order eighteen everywhere). This eliminates single-moment and one-signed
    quadratic tests as producers; the survivor is the sign-changing bilinear/deviation channel.
63. The half-density transport now identifies that channel source-first. The Möbius connection

64. Umbrella-reachable `XiShiftedHalfDensityAdjacentConnection.lean` resolves the formerly unnamed

65. Umbrella-reachable `XiResolventMassRigidity.lean` strengthens the stationary chart. It proves

    `(X+1)^4M(X)->0`, and decay along one escaping sequence suffices. This is a sharp persistence

66. Umbrella-reachable `XiShiftedCanonicalAdmissionRecessionConnection.lean` identifies normalized

67. Umbrella-reachable `XiResolventArithmeticTarget.lean` gives the exact stationary target

    ~~~
    sum_q ||u_q(X)||^2 = Re R_1(X) + 2 M(X).
    ~~~

    Hence the geometric side always dominates the arithmetic second moment, equality at any

    Quartic rigidity then makes every successful beat-quartic majorant a collapse certificate, not

68. Umbrella-reachable `XiResolventLineReflectionDefect.lean` identifies that same mass with one

    ~~~
    ||u_X-Ju_X||_2^2=4M(X),
    2M(X)=||u_X||_2^2-Q_Weil(u_X).
    ~~~

    At every nonnegative shift RH is equivalent to literal line-reflection fixedness. Both
    orientations lie in the same closed continued-Cauchy source carrier and admit independent
    separated finite-packet reconstruction on the same ray. Source closure therefore supplies
    both sides but cannot identify them. This merges U's stationary mass wall with the
    non-equivariant same-carrier wall isolated by R and L; it is a bridge/receiver, not a new
    route or an unconditional defect bound. Current working-tree strengthening proves the
    continued-Cauchy atoms are total in that closure and rewrites RH as equality of all unsquared
    holomorphic/reflected source polarizations. That coefficient-faithful reformulation remains
    RH-equivalent and receives only provisional credit.

**Open core.** Route U has two irreducible branches. In the degenerate/root-sign branch, items
55--58 replace unspecified odd descent by a literal Padé spectral-measure and source-capacity
statement. Define
`mu_j^+=sum_(u_(j,i)<0) (omega_(j,i)/x_(j,i)) delta_(-u_(j,i))`. Prove
`mu_j^+(K)->0` on every compact `K` in `(0,infinity)`, or the equivalent source-side vanishing of
the Poisson boundary atoms. This is weaker than full first-Hausdorff positivity: it permits
super-apex nodes whose residues collapse or whose hard coordinates escape. It excludes every
simple negative hard root with `gamma_r>0` by the exact residue limit. If `gamma_r=0`, use the
resulting parity-doubled terminal radical instead. Full odd-sector operator admission would still
require liminf control on the complete odd form domain.  Equivalently at a simple limiting root,
exclude a finite nonzero zero-edge response of the literal source `cosh(kappa t)`; forcing a
ground pole collapses the Padé residue but must then be combined with the explicit degeneracy
contradiction.  Equivalently, rule out the `sqrt(tau)` Lorentzian regularized-capacity spike
uniformly on every compact positive `kappa` interval.  Generic reduced-resolvent positivity,
pointwise control off the roots, and unscaled capacity averaging do not decide which alternative
occurs.

Odd or degenerate Connes--van Suijlekom extension is also closed as a stand-alone mechanism.
Positive terminal mass makes `i kappa` common only to the even kernel; it does not constrain an odd
lower-ground vector there.  Moreover item 59 proves that a simple odd lower ground is itself
Fourier-real-rooted, so odd descent supplies no contradiction.  A viable use of the full-ground
theorem must first prove from the literal completed-Xi source that the selected even boundary state
is the full bottom, or derive a new cross-parity identity forcing its nonreal zero onto every lower
odd ground.

Item 60b further removes a hidden premise: whenever the positive-mass branch really descends below
the even floor, the odd lower ground is automatically unique and simple.  Its Fourier transform is
real-rooted and its reciprocal gap is one exact cross-source current, but neither invariant forces
vanishing at the even imaginary root.  Any successor invoking odd-ground degeneracy or merely
assuming odd simplicity is therefore obsolete.

The positive-real Padé lane is now equally sharp.  Item 60 proves that ordinary convergence in
capacity is too weak by one conformal derivative: it sees `rho_tilde->0` automatically while the
physical atom is the normalized quantity `rho_tilde/(1+z)`.  Carathéodory positivity supplies a
new exact reserve law, but a bounded positive atom needs only a vanishing fraction of the large
left-pole reservoir at the critical degree scale.  A closing theorem must therefore control the
Julia/Carleson boundary atom and its left-reserve payment using the literal prime--Gamma source;
no finite matched-jet or unweighted disk-capacity theorem can suffice.  Item 60a also closes the
obvious finite-head repair: enlarging the analytic half-plane inserts asymptotically identical
opposite boundary jets, so depletion has no stand-alone Carathéodory leverage.

Contact orientation is closed as a mechanism: every first Padé defect gives the same signed first
nonzero derivative to every hard node, but a positive derivative at a flat face does not make its
level nonnegative. In the simple-birth branch, the parent radical-jet theorem already merges the
problem with the literal simple-even ground sign; more root division, contact parity, or generic
Laguerre--Polya theory is theatre unless it produces a new prime--Gamma/global-Xi anchor. Fixed-
degree arcsine recession, weak convergence, and bulk quadrature cannot close either branch because
a vanishing ordinary weight may retain nonzero absolute Padé residue after hard blow-up.

The current-Christoffel outgoing-ray route remains an alternate producer,
but items 51--52 close its fixed-mass comparison and cancel all explicit admission velocity from
the mass-varying source debt. Item 53 proves generic positive moment flow cannot control the
remaining odd defect-energy drift plus current norm flux. Only a label-sensitive prime--Gamma or
global completed-Xi law can revive this lane. It is not logically required for the selected least-defect sequence. Fixed-face plateau,
amplitude gauge, coordinate gauge, sub/superparabolic least-defect escape, and translation to
spectral infinity are closed.

A Stage-1 successor proposes an alternate U-native formulation: the parabolic degree lower bound
puts terminal positivity within one bounded relative mass interval, so after static root division
is exhausted the remaining datum is adjacent frozen-to-current admission transport on that bounded
interval. Item 51 closes arithmetic formation and every fixed-mass comparison. Missing theorem is
now a literal arithmetic prime--Gamma/global-Xi quantitative or sign law for the item-52 odd
defect-energy/norm-flux expression along the selected path; generic positive resolvent-flow
arguments are false by item 53. It
is therefore a live refinement of the same parity-admission wall, not a new route or a proved
replacement open core; its abstract root-deletion wrapper receives no formal credit.

**Dead subclasses.** Generic moment positivity, positive atoms, tied-power sectors, positive/root
location, stochastic order, factorial-normalized carriers, fixed corona continuous reporters,
Gamma lifetime, per-orbit three-point positivity, fixed angular sectors, endpoint-height control,
reflection aggregation, unweighted total pair energy, and a post-contact fourth-cumulant reserve
all admit exact counterexamples, merely transport existing sign, or are equivalent to the terminal
sign. Absolute monic margins, amplitude-only normalization, coordinate-dilated recession, and
generic Gauss positivity likewise do not control moving admission shape.

**Why independent.** U is a first-crossing extension problem on an adaptive finite null flag.
Route G instead needs a cofinal two-angle meromorphic source and Xi transfer; P needs one global
compact positive measure. Neither route's producer implies U's adaptive Euler--Gamma crossing
orientation.

## 5. Candidate routes not yet instantiated

### Post-J principal-relative current

Would be new route only after literal source object, non-coboundary incidence, and unconditional RH
handoff are constructed. Current concrete realization is R.

### XiC order-one explicit-formula organ [SHARED INFRASTRUCTURE — NOT A ROUTE]

The former growth frontier is closed in committed umbrella Lean. `CompletedZetaGrowth.lean` proves
the unconditional global order-one envelope `xi_growth_order_le_one`; centered coordinate change
gives the corresponding XiC bound. `XiHadamardGrowth.lean` proves quotient constancy and the exact
multiplicity-faithful product

~~~
XiC = XiC(0) * xiCanonicalProduct,
~~~

with no residual exponential factor. `XiZerosInfinite.lean` proves the completed divisor support
infinite, while `XiHeatShellMinimum.lean` and the canonical-product layer supply shell finiteness
and inverse-square summability. Thus growth, Hadamard factorization, and nontrivial-zero existence
are not open.

The prime side is also umbrella-reachable: `XiWardInteriorTraceSplit.lean` proves on `Re s>1`
that `logDeriv xi` is its two pole terms plus `logDeriv GammaR` minus the von Mangoldt L-series;
`XiGuinandWeilSymmetricContour.lean` identifies the centered bottom edge with the exact
prime--archimedean source, and finite-contour modules give the zero-residue receiver. Moreover,
umbrella-reachable `XiRelativeResolventTrace.lean` already gives the absolutely convergent
centered zero-side partial fraction in relative-resolvent form.

Umbrella-reachable `XiExplicitFormulaLogDeriv.lean` transports that formula to classical
coordinates and proves, wherever `xi(s) != 0`,

~~~
logDeriv xi(s)
  = sum_rho (1/(rho-1/2) + 1/(s-rho)),
~~~

and glues it on `Re s>1` to the pole--Gamma--von-Mangoldt side as an unconditional zero-sum =
prime-sum identity. This direct classical-coordinate explicit formula is now formally certified;
it is shared infrastructure, not a missing proof route. Only a live route's independent
source-side positivity, coercivity, or cancellation theorem could turn these identities into RH
leverage.

Umbrella-reachable `XiWeilResolventChartDictionary.lean` now gives an exact base-chart weld. At
imaginary Cauchy parameter `-it`, each Weil pair
summand is the resolvent point at shift `t^2`; hence the diagonal Weil value is the zeroth
resolvent moment. Lean derives its strict positive real part, identifies it for `t>0`
with `logDeriv xi(1/2+t)/t`, and for `t>1/2` with the explicit pole--Gamma--von-Mangoldt
expression. These declarations receive formal credit. They orient only this Cauchy-test diagonal,
not the full Weil form or higher resolvent Hankel tower, so they weld C1/Weil and resolvent
infrastructure without closing a route or creating Route V.

Umbrella-reachable `XiResolventTowerLowDegreePositivity.lean` extends this weld formally. It places every shifted squared spectral point
in the open 45-degree cone for nonnegative shift and in the required 30-degree cone for shifts at
least `3/4`. Hence the first resolvent moment has strictly positive real part for every
nonnegative shift and the second does so for shifts at least `3/4`; an exact power-kernel
dictionary transfers these statements to squared and cubed Cauchy-test Weil diagonals. This is not
full Weil positivity: degree two at small shift and the higher tower remain unoriented. These
declarations receive formal credit but do not change any route endpoint.

### Order-one pre-Gamma Sonin successor

Would replace F's wrong-order finite-height transfer. No source operator and chain exist yet.

### Conservative Euler-shell/Witt scale completion

This is the closest new Route-V candidate at the cutoff, but it is not yet instantiated. Lean
constructs literal Möbius prime-exponent formation edges, their conservation/reconstruction laws,
polar criticality, and a bilateral-summability selector whose surviving radial character is
unitary. It also constructs pole-unit memory, the Gamma base, and the Euler-shell specialization.
The conservative inverse-completion theorem is exact: if finite formation and exterior companion
stages are inverse pairs and converge jointly in one Hausdorff multiplicative completion, the
inverse identity survives, so every multiplicative receiver sees a zero-free completed formation.
The literal pointwise Euler-shell specialization is now Lean-proved and classified exactly:
for every `m>=12`, pointwise conservative completion on the open right half-strip is equivalent to
RH. This is a terminal gate, not a source theorem; it deliberately forgets that the stages must be
source-formed and converge jointly in one completion.

The missing source object is decisive: no committed theorem constructs the finite source-generated
inverse pair and proves its joint convergence on the half-plane needed by the endpoint. A further
no-go forbids hiding this in one ordinary Banach algebra with a bounded derivation satisfying
`D T=1`; the viable completion must be scale-, sheaf-, or locally convex. Until those literal
stages and their joint admission are built, this is a proved receiver architecture rather than an
independent route.

### Triptic orbital-divisor normal-form successor

Lean now gives a complete finite source quotient. Every mixed-constraint periodic meromorphic
source differs from an explicit finite elliptic-gamma jet correction by a differentially
algebraic meromorphic remainder, and every finite orbital divisor datum is realized. The additive
divisor is a finite class function with one jet basis element per orbit class and order. This is a
source-classification theorem, not merely special-function notation. The torus invariant-field
layer is also complete: arbitrary protocols retain velocity plus annihilating Laurent monomials,
while an expanding protocol leaves only a differentially independent velocity whose intersection
with palette rational functions is constant. The concrete presentation gap is closed as well:
elliptic-gamma cone and multiplicative presentations agree after the explicit coordinate shift,
meromorphic realization commutes with translation, logarithmic derivative, and every jet, and any
nonzero finite jet combination, including the untranslated base-source class, is formally
hypertranscendental. The remaining kernel ambiguity is now closed: a source is differentially
algebraic exactly when every additive orbital-divisor coefficient vanishes; for multiplicative
sources, its logarithmic derivative is differentially algebraic exactly when every triptic orbit
charge vanishes.

No committed theorem maps this quotient coefficient-faithfully to the Xi divisor or supplies a
reflection/polarization law forcing critical orientation. Those two constructions would instantiate
a genuinely new route because its first producer would be orbital-divisor exhaustion, not a
classical positivity or scalar criterion. At this cutoff it remains a second Route-V candidate.

### Tropical-projective source-normal descent

Umbrella Lean now gives a third Route-V candidate core. For any finite nonempty family of exact
source actions, mandatory maximum work has a nonnegative projective width; width vanishes exactly
when every parent increment agrees, is invariant under a common potential, and controls descent of
the full relative action profile through an observer. Closed histories have nonnegative work. For
finite prime clocks, arbitrarily late phase returns coexist with projective width growing at least
linearly with elapsed time and the logarithmic clock span. For a centered affine parameter and a
nonzero clock span, zero width is exactly the centered state.

This is not Route V. The centered-width theorem does not mention Xi, and the recurrence theorem
does not force observer descent. A route would require one source-normal Xi observer constructed
before divisor specialization, proof that the complete prime projective profile factors through
it at every Xi zero, and a coefficient-faithful identification of its zero-width fibre with the
physical centered state. No such observer, descent theorem, or RH handoff exists.

### Nyman--Beurling / Baez--Duarte finite sections

New Lean corpus formalizes criterion, Gram matrices, detectors, least squares, finite-section
determinants, Möbius residuals, and obstructions. This is important infrastructure but classical
receiver architecture. No new unconditional approximation producer was found; it is not counted
as Route V.

### Adaptive eigenframe source memory

Umbrella Lean now proves exact two-query Pauli twirling, Haar mean/tail laws, and permanent
fixed-body Majorana separation: for every fixed positive even body order, every nonzero autonomous
Hamiltonian with that support stays strictly above the Haar score at every time once qubit count
is large enough. The generator-matched form is stronger and dimension-only: every nonzero
traceless autonomous `n`-qubit Hamiltonian retains its full adjoint autocorrelation at every time,
whereas Haar probability of retaining even half that amplitude is at most
`4/((2^n)^2-1)`; the dictionary signal and Haar separation hold simultaneously. This is a genuine
autonomous source-memory mechanism. It becomes an RH route only after constructing an arithmetic
Xi-typed Hamiltonian or source, preserving divisor and multiplicity provenance, and proving a
coefficient-faithful handoff to an RH endpoint. None exists at this cutoff, so it is a candidate
grammar rather than Route V.

### Longitudinal--transverse source selection gate

Umbrella Lean now decomposes each complete-resolution source into a marked longitudinal divisor
coordinate and a zero-codiagonal transverse radical. For every continuous linear intervention,
the output codiagonal is exactly its marked-section response plus a uniquely defined mixing defect.
Vanishing mixing is equivalent to preserving the radical; with scalar section action, such an
intervention can only rescale the already-present divisor. The literal complete-shell
specialization proves that arbitrary transverse perturbations leave the pole-unit scale response
unchanged.

This is a cross-route mechanism law, not Route V. It rules out normalized shell manipulation,
radical-preserving completion, or transverse source enrichment as a selector by itself. A new route
could begin only with a source-generated intervention having nonzero longitudinal--transverse
mixing, followed by reflected analytic admission and a coefficient-faithful Xi handoff. No such
intervention is instantiated.

### Cofinal port, inertia, and phase-memory receivers

New umbrella theorems identify limiting inverse capacity with admission, its zero set with the
closed vertical fibre, and closability with nondegeneracy; divergent weighted capacity gives dense
kernel. The prime port separates a finite zero jet from a divergent first prime-log jet. Complete
atomic fusion preserves the sum of Hermitian inertias, so negative channels cannot disappear under
full interpolation. Prime augmentation reduces to one hyperbolic plane and supplies no independent
Witt-rank obstruction. Finite lattice directions force rational verticality, but a cofinally
rotating lattice can converge to an irrational vertical line, excluding finite-direction
extrapolation. Finite unitary/Choi/Pauli and phase-action laws retain source memory, but an
alternating binary character reaches the phase maximum; phase complexity alone cannot select RH.
These results sharpen L/R/U receiver and no-go categories but have no Xi-typed producer and are not
Route V.

### Source-coherent multi-organ synchronization

Lean now proves a finite selection grammar that survives independent-organ gauge no-gos. Pairwise
relative responses reconstruct every local intervention modulo one global source action; one
anchor completes reconstruction. For a family of source response maps, synchronization selects
zero exactly when their difference equalizer is trivial. Combined with shared-gauge quotient
fusion, one global prime packet produces a nonnegative pairwise slope-variance defect conserved
through partitions and received by Boolean, mass, Hankel, and quantum-information charts.

This is not Route V. No committed theorem constructs Xi-typed organ maps whose divisor
admissibility forces synchronization, and no arithmetic proof makes their equalizer trivial on the
horizontal displacement. Those two source-first statements would instantiate a new architecture;
the generic finite theorem does not.

### Euler q-Gamma divisor-translation process

This is a real new source architecture but not yet a proof route. It begins with positive
prime-local q-Gamma laws, produces an ordinary random-integer law, continues its global response
meromorphically, and lands every zeta-zero multiplicity as the exact Cartier-divisor jump under
unit translation. The cyclic GNS observer is recurrent and has compact diagonal resolvent. The
response and its continuations are now Lean-proved hypertranscendental. The genus-one exchange is
also fully certified: renormalized local factors are quadratically close to one, their prime
product is multipliable, locally uniformly convergent, holomorphic and zero-free on
`Re(s)>1/(2 sigma)-1`, and the natural renormalization erases the translation divisor.

The abelian zeta-gas layer supplies its pre-zero source. Prime-power geometric modes give the
zeta-Gibbs law on finite abelian groups; order/temperature dilation and socle/fugacity translation
are distinct response axes, and the q-Gamma random-integer law is the exact socle shadow. Lean
certifies the coupled response ratio, depth-shift telescoping, depth-dilation semigroup, depth
condensation walls, local and global socle law, mode and gas expectations, and the additive
von-Mangoldt/`-zeta'/zeta` residue chain. The full response has a finite-depth divisor formula and
a convergent, nonzero, holomorphic depth tail, hence an exact meromorphic order formula away from
the natural accumulation boundary. It also proves that accumulation line is a genuine natural
boundary, constructs the gas word/KMS algebra with a unique admissible Gibbs state, and realizes
depth dilation as an injective RG embedding. On the class space Lean gives atomic mutual absolute
continuity, the canonical tilt density and Radon--Nikodym/log-density formulas, measurability of
the class map, equality of class law with the gas pushforward, and socle expectation transport.
The entropy layer is now closed too: log-socle and both log-likelihood ratios are integrable, both
directed Kullback--Leibler divergences are evaluated, and their symmetrized value is exactly
`sigma` times the von-Mangoldt Dirichlet mass, equivalently the corresponding logarithmic zeta
derivative expression. The umbrella-reachable squarefull-sector module identifies the depth-two
wall at `1/2` and constructs the corresponding almost surely finite random abelian-group law;
this is a source phase boundary, not a zero-location theorem, because both order and activity
observers remain divisor-blind. The Gibbs Gram kernel, positivity on `a* a`, product-reversing star,
completed GNS representation, generated C-star algebra, positive cyclic vector state, and its
continuous normalized KMS uniqueness on the whole generated C-star algebra are now all
umbrella-reachable. The exact modulus analysis is now also formal at umbrella level: the complete
nonnegative depth-by-harmonic defect ladder is summable, equals the q-Pochhammer/q-Gamma
log-modulus defect, has leading term `q(1-cos theta)`, and differs from it by a remainder summable
over primes throughout `sigma>1/2`. Vacuum-weight bounds give probability at least `q/2` to one
copy empty and the other occupied, and prove these symmetrization probabilities are not summable
for `1/2<sigma<=1`. This is the exact Borel--Cantelli divergence input, not yet its product-measure
wrapper or a zero-location theorem. The modulus-budget module is now umbrella-reachable: phase
gauge leaves modulus unchanged, while the genus-one counterterm pays the leading defect up to an
absolutely summable correction for `sigma>1/2`. The wired prime-window theorem proves PNT in fixed
multiplicative/phase windows, divergence of the harmonic window sum, and nonsummability of the
leading defect for every nonzero phase when `0<sigma<=1`. The now-wired continuation transfers
this divergence to the full q-Gamma defect, proves its partial sums tend to infinity, the
phase-gauged product norm tends to zero for `1/2<sigma<=1`, and no continuous centering limit can
remain normalized at phase zero. These results supply a genuine
positive operator-algebraic source and a sharp probabilistic/analytic continuation boundary, not
a critical-line orientation.

A new law-control layer extracts a marked source commutator from this gas. Lean now proves that
exchanging temperature translation with source-selected socle tilt generates the pure fugacity
parameter translation. Lean also proves the socle response quotient, its exact translation law,
safe-chamber holomorphy/zero-freeness, hypertranscendence, and complete finite-order divisor law.
Positive fugacity entropy, recurrence/open lift, and any critical-line handoff remain open. The
mechanism is explicitly not RH.

A second comparison prevents overgeneralizing that commutator mechanism. The gas realizes
law genesis by noncommuting endpoint schedules, whereas the elliptic-gamma source has commuting
endpoint translations and trivial pure two-shift Koszul defect but a nonzero mixed
coefficient/Čech transgression. The proposed common grammar must therefore retain execution
exchange and coefficient/admission lift as separate grades. The two-source capstone
`graded_source_genesis_noncollapse` is now umbrella-reachable: it proves noncommuting gas schedules
and commuting triptic translations coexist with a non-coboundary gamma-gerbe `3`-cocycle. It has
no typed Xi handoff. This is a field-level architecture constraint, not a new lettered route.

What is missing is not another landing or special-function theorem. No source law relates these
two observer axes, recurrence, compact resolvent, hypertranscendence, or translation divisor to
`Re rho=1/2`. Until a source-derived critical-line orientation and unconditional RH handoff are
constructed, this remains Route-S infrastructure and is not Route V.

### Gauss quasi-inner multi-place assembly

This candidate has advanced from a formal finite-place sketch to a complete local asymptotic
mechanism. For each Gauss component the umbrella constructs the actual circle operators, proves
uniform moving-amplitude translation, exact principal factorization, controlled finite iteration,
and convergence of the quasi-inner diagonal coefficient to its closed period average along every
cofinal integer-frequency sequence. The full scaled two-frequency diagonal is now
umbrella-reachable. The sandwich fold and relative-diagonal bound isolate the principal quadratic
form with a vanishing dimensionless defect. The exact local logarithmic residue for the actual
operator in the rank-one case is now umbrella-reachable. For `m>=2`, the umbrella proves the
ratio-weighted moving-translation defect for every decay exponent above `1/2`, so the former
tail-exponent wall is closed. It also constructs the literal principal step, proves the exact
Fourier-block identity for its powers, and supplies the general operator-power telescope. The first propagated-iterate depth is now closed in umbrella Lean: the relative one-step law and
the double-defect estimate hold for every `m >= 2`, and the operator logarithmic-mean residue law
holds at `m = 2`. The remaining local theorem is propagated-iterate control at depth two and
beyond (`m >= 3`). A potential
new architecture would assemble these component laws over all places into the actual
Connes--Consani intersection/Weil pairing and extract a positive or oriented global source law.
The label-faithful multi-place assembly now exists in umbrella Lean: a block-diagonal operator
on the `ℓ²`-direct sum of the per-place circle spaces with identically vanishing cross-place
matrix elements at every power, per-label localized residue laws realized by the actual
operators at `m ≤ 2`, exact codiagonal collapse onto `gaussPairedChannelResidue` (so the paired
Schatten layer is the label-blind shadow), sample-value conjugation and phase evenness retained
per label, a canonical injective cofinal prime aperture, the Weil normalization
`residue·log p = (2/m²)((p-1)/p)^{2m}(p^m-1)²`, and a strictly positive residue current in the
horizontal carrier's `(Primes -> R)` label bundle that preserves the carrier's sign and critical
radical at every label. The assembled current now has its local Hodge pairing:
`GaussHodgeMassCurrent` combines tail-log and prime-Bockstein odd components into a
reflection-even nonnegative mass with exact critical radical and proves total vanishing equivalent
to RH. Thus local positivity/orientation is closed. What remains absent is an independent global
trace/explicit-formula identity forcing this positive mass to vanish, together with identification
of that identity with the literal Connes--Consani intersection/Weil object. The interface itself

### Cassini--Wold / Deninger lower-half weld

This umbrella-reachable architecture is a real opening, but route-equivalence is already proved.
The Stieltjes--Cassini defect is a finite nonnegative sum that vanishes
exactly under RH; its phase-clock frequency is the mixed horizontal-grade/physical-height source
jet, and its BV--Wold landing is an exact positive Fisher energy. At critical half density, a
bounded Euler coframe removes the apparent compact Wold smoothing. The valuation-one Route-T
current then equals a pure Wold half-density current plus a uniformly bounded trace-class
correction, and the two line-bound propositions are equivalent.

Ordered Abel summation splits the pure Wold current into an explicit logarithmic-mesh current, one
static first-prime holonomy atom, and a residual connection. The residual arithmetic primitive
tends to zero with an exponential square-root-log rate. Its square is summable against the exact
critical harmonic prime weight, so it is an unconditional finite-Fisher-energy vector; pointwise
damping is still too weak for total-variation closure. Independently, the unsquared theta
resolvent row and singular-metric two-port Green identity express the same current; theta-Green
plus adjacent-prime/Wold current cancels to a bounded dispersive background with an explicit
two-residue future carrier. Exterior reflection does not yield a second equation: reversing the
safe theta line and frequency negates the complete packet exactly and preserves its norm.

The physical observer is now classified. Its coefficient `p+1` is unbounded on the Fisher carrier,
so residual-only bounded same-carrier landing is impossible; nevertheless it is exactly the legal
lower contour shift by `-i/2`. The induced residual connection converges absolutely and is
`C^infinity` with every polynomial log moment. Negative-frequency polarization now extends it
holomorphically through the entire lower half-plane, and division by the coordinate gives a global
Hardy bound. The main logarithmic mesh is an exact right-endpoint quadrature whose
error reduces to the classical mean-square prime-gap sum.

This does not instantiate Route V. Lean now proves the exact two-sided mesh criterion: at critical
depth, absolute mesh-defect convergence is equivalent to the weighted consecutive-prime-gap
energy. That critical energy monotonically controls the whole complex mesh-defect series on
`-1/2 <= Re s <= 0`, including every shifted zero in `1/2 <= Re rho <= 1`; imaginary height changes
only a fixed prefactor. Formalizing the published mean-square prime-gap theorem would therefore
close the entire mesh half-strip input, but not Route T. Lean also proves the exact safe Laplace
symbol, holomorphy of every valuation
`>= 2` contribution on the critical half-plane, meromorphic continuation of the valuation-one
Wold carrier, and a nonzero residue at the translated location of every off-critical zeta zero;
later shifts and higher valuations cannot cancel it. Surviving scientific work is source-side
continuation/noncancellation of the complete prime-zeta-minus-mesh-minus-holonomy identity toward
`Re s=-1/2`, or the equivalent finite-stage source/divisor cancellation through the cofinal
limit. The reflected two-line scalar quadrature is closed and should not be pursued.

The former two-sided residual shortcut is closed negatively. Its critical endpoint graph norm
would force the same prime-gap energy while opening the residual half-strip, but item 51 proves
that combination impossible because it erases genuine critical Wold poles. The published
prime-gap import is therefore not optional on any surviving absolute-mesh lane.

An umbrella-reachable Abel-threshold module closes the local bridge from
prime-gap moments to this mesh. If the prefix gap-square moment grows like `p_M^theta` for any
`theta<3/2`, the critical energy is summable; the concrete `theta=11/8` specialization closes the
whole complex half-strip mesh. Thus the only literature-to-Lean debt in this component is the
published unconditional consecutive-prime-gap moment itself in the required prefix form. Neither
supplies the remaining Hardy/Wold noncancellation theorem.

### Tight-source coalitional / collective-repair architecture

The new universal-law theorems expose another possible producer grammar. Positive-carrier
attainment can be attacked by proving tightness or bounded proper pricing for finite source
coalitions, while semantically conservative repairs can create a positive circulation only through
an irreducibly collective threshold invisible to each repair separately. A new RH route would have
to define the arithmetic defects and repair interaction before observing zero locations, prove the
tightness/threshold law from the prime--Gamma source, and map the born circulation
coefficient-faithfully to an RH endpoint. None of these arithmetic objects exists in the corpus.
The cofinal viability layer adds a sharp promotion law: compact observer fibres and closed coherent
horizons make finite survival promote to one global source witness; failure forces every witness
out of every compact set and makes every predeclared proper source cost diverge. Compact legal
control fibres also make the endogenous predecessor commute with the cofinal intersection and
produce the greatest viable fixed law. This rules out false finite-horizon immortality as a
producer unless noncompact escape, nonclosed admission, or incoherent horizons are used. No
arithmetic observer, horizon, or Xi evaluation is instantiated. This is a route-design opening,
not Route V.

The new symbolic-execution capstone closes another false promotion. Executable grade-`m` repair
always implies the local failure inequalities, and for one failure kernel the converse holds. On
the three-kernel triangle, however, uniform weight `n` is locally admitted at grade `2n` but cannot
decompose into `2n` legal grade-one repairs; these nonexecutable grades are unbounded while the
normalized profile and parity observer stay constant. Thus local Hall/failure inequalities plus a
bounded normalized observer do not certify an executable arithmetic source law. Any route from
this opening must build the actual compatible decomposition/history, not only its symbolic cone.
No such arithmetic execution system or Xi handoff exists, so the theorem narrows the candidate
grammar rather than creating a route.

The primitive-execution refinement now gives the exact surviving invariant. Every positive
locally admitted repair has a canonical primitive decomposition; bounded grammar is equivalent to
bounded primitive degree. Triangle repair is generated in degree two, but the cofinal
complete-complement family has primitive degree `k-1`, so every bounded grammar misses an explicit
admitted law. Primitive degree spectrum is invariant under coordinate relabeling and under the
failure-antichain quotient. The exact primitive ray executes, its projective profile and cyclic
grade observer are periodic, yet its exact grade advances without bound. Thus a viable arithmetic
execution route needs a cofinal primitive spectrum or genuinely unbounded grammar; bounded
observer data cannot promote it. No prime--Gamma repair family or Xi handoff is instantiated, so
this is a sharper route-design opening, not a new route.

Positive observer transport preserves both symbolic admission and actual execution and therefore
cannot manufacture an execution gap. A noninjective triangle observer can nevertheless erase a
real source gap while creating false positive overlap. Observer collapse is therefore another
one-way information-loss mechanism, not a repair producer.

### HTGeometry universal-law-field and analytic receiver infrastructure

New umbrella-reachable modules prove compact-carrier representation for continuous functionals on
compact-open map spaces, dense finite-support prime-quadrant tangent graphs, local
Poisson/Stieltjes absolute-continuity criteria, finite-matrix spectral-density decay and Dyson
identities, exact separable Bures/Alberti approximation and compact-target minimax laws, explicit
finite-amplifier pattern/width caps, the formal parts of the abelian zeta gas, and full finite
one-replica Pauli generation. The now-wired two-query eigenframe module adds exact Pauli twirling,
Haar mean and tail, and for every fixed positive even body order an asymptotic permanent strict
separation: every nonzero autonomous Hamiltonian with that Majorana support stays above the Haar
score at every time. Its new generator-matched theorem removes the body-order condition: every
nonzero traceless autonomous Hamiltonian retains full adjoint autocorrelation for all time, while
the Haar half-energy tail is at most `4/(D^2-1)`. It has no Xi-typed source or handoff and is not a
route. The
wired modules also prove source-context derivative and challenge-transduction laws,
observer rank contraction with exact hidden-kernel witnesses, Gorenstein/monogenic residual-fibre
and congruence-order residue, first-order, Kähler-different, cotangent/trace-dual, and closed-fibre
source-linear comparison structure, together with the commutative Shilov boundary-ideal
dictionary. A separate spectral-rigidity theorem shows that when a finite algebraic spectrum has
only the trace rational relation, deleting one energy makes its exponentials algebraically
independent; tracelessness recovers the deleted exponential by the product-one law. Relation-space
rank is now exactly `D-1`, nonzero overlaps are necessary for Krylov cyclicity, and the spectral
family is connected to the exact circuit lower bound `D-1 <= gateCount*q`. This has no Xi source
specialization satisfying the rigidity and preparation premises. The closed-hyperplane inverse,
compact perturbation/adjoint infrastructure, and Hedenmalm specialization are umbrella-reachable.
They give generic closed dense inverses, exact adjoint domains, natural adjoint squares, compact
zero-resolvent, every closed theta translate on one smooth core, the full conormal hyperplane and

self-adjoint supersymmetric block, exact kernel/range, Fredholm index, and Witten index are also
formal. Shifted cokernel and operator geometry are therefore closed; spectral reality and
orientation are not. The
operator-system layer is no longer scalar-only: it now has matricial orderings,
amplification/compression, completely positive maps, scalar-order-unit to matrix-order-unit
promotion, matrix level norms, the Ruan direct-sum law, and isometric compression contractivity.
The locally C*-algebra layer constructs projective limits of complete C*-algebras, proves closedness
and completeness, the coordinate seminorm C*-identity, and stability of the bounded part.
The commutative boundary-existence gap is now discharged for genuine function systems:
under the unit, star-closure, and point-separation hypotheses, Lean proves the Choquet boundary is
norming, lies in every closed boundary, closed boundaries are intersection-stable, and the Shilov
boundary is the least closed boundary and equals the closure of the Choquet boundary. The new
commutative completion modules also identify the Shilov ideal as the greatest boundary ideal,

noncommutative arithmetic envelope.
Critical-locus quadruple evenness supplies another universal no-go: every
permutation-invariant functional of one off-line splitting quadruple is even in horizontal
displacement, so its first variation at the critical locus vanishes and the second central jet is
the first possible signal. These are substantial
generic source and receiver theorems, but none has a typed map to a live route's first unresolved
arithmetic theorem.

The analytic-algebraic infrastructure now includes the full root-existence form of
Newton--Puiseux. Umbrella Lean proves that every positive-degree monic polynomial over `K[[t]]`,
for algebraically closed characteristic-zero `K`, acquires a power-series root after one finite
ramification `t=s^N`. The chain includes exact minimal positive Newton slope, Tschirnhaus removal,
multiplicity factorization, ramification composition, the Hensel--Rychlik defect-root theorem, and
complete factorization after one finite ramification. Generic finite matrix-word circuits have an
umbrella-reachable polynomial parametrization and image theorem; under the stated algebraic-
independence hypothesis their compact-time orbit intersections are finite. It also includes the coefficient recursion that drives Eisenstein's algebraicity theorem and
integer power-series tail/logarithm bounds. The full Eisenstein denominator theorem is now wired:
coefficient denominators have a geometric bound, and the simple-root, unramified, ramified,
defect-reduction, uniform, and fraction-field scaling cases are assembled without an integrality
hypothesis. Rational algebraic branches can now be rescaled to integral power-series models and

of such images only finitely often under the stated algebraic-independence hypotheses. These are

specialization maps their root or denominator data to a live route's missing sign, annihilator,
or carrier.

Critical-cover algebra has advanced in parallel. Under full actual geometric monodromy, the
embedded source-field stabilizer is maximal and there are no proper intermediate fields below the
source field; a shared nonzero coprime tangent rigidly identifies two critical fibre states; and a
Schur reduction with at most two colliding sheets forces every inertia element to be identity or a

integral-closure model is now wired: it constructs the contracted nonzero maximal prime, identifies
