zero, and identifies inertia cardinality with the ramification index. These theorems sharpen Route S's possible
algebraic landing, but full monodromy/collision premises are not an RH orientation and no Xi
handoff is instantiated.

The derived-selection and constraint-relativity families sharpen the negative classification.
Their Riemann pullback is nontrivial exactly on the critical line, so using it as a selector is an
RH-equivalent receiver. Faithful re-encoding, fixed defect closure, bounded/static observers,
unframed rank-one naturality, symmetric source inheritance, and finite word filtrations cannot
promote the missing law. A common multiplicative regularizer is also formally blind to subleading
differences between systems sharing one nonzero leading term: convergence for one forces same
regularized limit for the other. Compact-carrier existence therefore does not construct P's
positive source measure; dense prime tangents do not supply a singular sign; Bures minimax and
the formal zeta-gas laws do not supply arithmetic incidence.

New umbrella capstones make the observer-collapse boundary explicit. Scalar, magnitude, and
support are dependent shadows of one framed source and their product cannot select; a bounded
fixed-code observer cannot equal an unbounded wormhole length; difference, discriminant/Fitting
energy, support kernel, and conditioning are four shadows of one quadratic law. Question/relation
balance and the rank/depth/orientation/discriminant transmutation dictionary are exact. Finally,
longer words for one fixed action never recreate freedom already killed by the action: once the
filtration plateaus, new selection requires a changed action, module, quotient, admission law, or
source. Clone re-encoding now includes nontrivial full/projection clone and top/bottom congruence
extremes and proves their transport stability; it confirms that faithful re-encoding preserves a
nontrivial law lattice rather than producing a new selector. These classifications prevent
recounting dependent reporters as new route pressure.

The generative-response field now also has exact finite mechanics for coherent versus positive
history, marked-variation exchange, calibration spectra, compact globalization of common normers,
cofinal admission escape, finite Fourier-kernel order reflection, complete test-coordinate fusion,
amplification-depth escape, and Witt/Adams formation memory. Serial positive observation can kill
a symbolic-execution gap only once and cannot revive it. These are route-design and no-promotion
theorems: none supplies a prime--Gamma source family, Xi-typed landing, or a premise that closes a
live route.

Its umbrella-reachable longitudinal--transverse capstone makes one shared obstruction exact. The
complete source splits into the scalar pole-unit coordinate plus a zero-codiagonal radical, and
every intervention output splits into marked-section response plus mixing defect. Radical
preservation is exactly zero mixing and permits only scalar rescaling of the longitudinal divisor;
even endpoint-dependent transverse perturbations are invisible to the complete-resolution scale
response. Hence any formation-based RH mechanism needs a source-generated nonzero mixing gate
before divisor specialization, not more normalized transverse structure.

The global-formation properness modules now supply a genuine pre-zero selection law inside the
abelian zeta gas. Depth dilation amplifies every nonneutral fugacity formation; the least-prime,
least-depth Bernoulli reporter has exact linearly coercive work; the full group-class Jeffreys work
dominates it by data processing. Bounded work along the complete scale orbit is therefore
equivalent to neutral formation. This closes generic formation generation, reporter coercivity,
and full-source information properness. It does not identify a physical Xi off-center coordinate
with the formation parameter, intertwine Xi depth transport with this dilation, or prove the
actual landed source work uniformly bounded. Without those maps it is a candidate producer

### External Zeta-23 finite-compression density theorem

Claude's 10 August 2026 paper *More than two thirds of the zeros of the Riemann zeta function lie
on the critical line*, with its public `zeta-23-lean` artifact, adds a genuine unconditional
finite-Weil theorem: critical-density Gabor compressions, unconditional prime-side trace and
Frobenius-square asymptotics, and a multiplicity-aware Hermitian rank--trace inequality yield
critical-line/simple proportions `2/3` and distinct proportion `5/6` (optimized to approximately
`0.6725`, `0.6725`, `0.83625`). The external Lean artifact states these results directly against
Mathlib's `riemannZeta`; local source audit found no forbidden declarations under its theorem
library, while independent replay of its different Lean/Mathlib toolchain has not been performed in
this corpus. Detailed disposition is in `claude-zeta23-rank-trace-audit.md`.

This does not add a lettered RH route or close the R/L/CCM producer. The scalar compression has
already codiagonalized prime labels and reflection orientations, and its normalized trace moments
cannot exclude one, finitely many, or density-zero off-line blocks. That limitation coincides with
the corpus's formal `MeasuredIndexBlindness` wall.

A hostile follow-up closes the proposed moment-neutral amplification escape. The source-horizontal
prime tail already amplifies every off-line multiplicity copy into arbitrarily many distinct,
nonzero, reflection-faithful hyperbolic blocks with arbitrarily small average Frobenius-square cost;
this is formalized in `SourceHorizontalMomentNeutralAmplification`. It cannot contradict the
paper's inequality: each block adds one positive-index unit, and the rank--trace certificate allows
`c^2` per such unit. The paper's equality examples make that price sharp. Requiring every block to
remain above a fixed spectral threshold forces at least `2 delta^2` Frobenius square per block, so
sublinear moment growth is possible only through spectral collapse. Bounded source operations
preserve the collapse; unbounded sign/projection normalization has simultaneous forward/reflected
domain exactly at neutral grade and therefore restates the existing RH-equivalent producer. Detailed
proof and conjecture loop: `source-horizontal-moment-neutral-amplification-no-go.md`.

The umbrella-reachable shared-reservoir successor preserves every prime coefficient while coupling
all blocks through one negative reservoir. It realizes exact opposite inertias with arbitrarily
small Frobenius square, but also proves that an off-line packet and its reflection cannot both
embed quadratic-form-faithfully in any coefficient-generated global Stokes Witt form: that target
has only one negative port. Thus the amplification data now yield a sharp Route-R landing
criterion, not a contradiction with the finite-compression density theorem.

## 6. Fundamental nature of remaining problems

Across live routes, first unresolved statements fall into five mathematical classes.

| Class | Routes | Missing knowledge |
|---|---|---|
| singular source descent | C1, L, R, CCM-FU | positive closure or coefficient-faithful annihilation before scalar quotient; R/L additionally need a constitutive coefficient selector because vertical-gauge invariance erases and marked splitting is arbitrary |
| all-prime oscillatory transport | D2, E, T | cancellation or finite ceiling retaining arithmetic phase and physical normalization; T's safe symbol, Wold pole formation, and mesh-gap equivalence are formal, leaving continuation/noncancellation of the complete identity toward the physical wall |
| adaptive spectral orientation | G, U | source-conditioned sign on moving boundary/null data; U's first singular face and every fixed positive even contact are Lean-classified, leaving cofinal contact-order/degree drift |
| whole-space coercivity | O | classify all low-energy ground-space escape directions, not one test vector; N's analogous receiver has been terminalized |
| positive carrier existence | P, Q | build literal compact measure after coupled pole--zeta--Gamma cancellation |

This explains repeated stalls. Classical functional analysis supplies receivers once sign,
boundedness, or coercivity is present. Corpus no-gos show these properties are not inherited from
ambient positivity, faithful re-encoding, local exactness, finite moments, bounded observer
towers, isometry, compact-carrier representation, or determinant packaging. The shared horizontal
carrier theorem sharpens this diagnosis: retained class and its orientation are already present;
missing theorem is an arithmetic law forcing its annihilation. More generally, decisive theorem
must act on source before completion removes reflection-odd, phase-sensitive, or adaptive boundary
coordinate. The exact longitudinal--transverse law sharpens “source selection” further: decisive
operation must mix zero-codiagonal source information into longitudinal divisor response; a
radical-preserving intervention can only rescale what was already there.

Every retired route remains retired unless new work changes primitive or defeats its exact no-go.

## 8. Literature exchange and novelty boundary

Primary literature supplies parent languages but not current Tier-1 source theorems.

- Hedenmalm's 2026 differential-pair spectral realization gives a new spectral interpretation of
  zeta zeros, but no T outgoing-current bound, N source-domain estimate, or U null-flag
  orientation: https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17494
- Suzuki's 2026 screw-function formulation gives finite-interval self-adjoint operators and a
  conjectural limiting program, but no source-selected G transfer or U completed-source crossing
  sign: https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09096
- Berg--Szwarc characterize Hankel determinant/positivity subtleties; determinant nonnegativity
  does not supply PSD of every adaptive U face: https://arxiv.org/abs/1405.7267
- Salazar obtains total positivity from positive moment representations; this does not orient
  signed adaptive discrepancy after shifted-Xi factorial normalization:
  https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31647
- Matomäki--Radziwiłł--Tao's averaged Chowla/Elliott theorem and Menon's 2026 refinement give the
  relevant external correlation technology. Lean now proves the required small/large truncation
  bridge without a maximum over all endpoints. Remaining import is the exact uniform Möbius
  specialization, including nonpretentious hypotheses and rates, of the published theorem:
  https://arxiv.org/abs/1503.05121 and https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15574

Scoped searches found no exact external collision for T's complete mass-light-cone reduction or
U's Hilbert--charge/null-flag/Lorentz chain. This is evidence for corpus novelty, not proof of
historical priority. Specialist review remains required.

External results worth formalizing must be unconditional, source-specific, absent from corpus, and
must narrow first open premise. Additional abstract RH criteria, generic spectral models, or
moment theorems do not meet this bar.

## 9. Disproof frontier

One rigorously certified off-line Xi zero disproves RH. Equivalent finite negative witnesses are
already formalized through restricted-Weil floors, continued prime--archimedean tails, Hankel
faces, and several determinant criteria. No such witness exists in corpus.

Most explicit source-level disproof candidate is growing-order squared-Xi reverse-Bessel radial
jet. Orders through eighteen are positive; moving-prime comparison from order nineteen onward is
open. Numerical evidence is not accepted as proof or disproof.

   ground-residue reflection cone, then resolve the singular negative zero-shift boundary layer
   `r->0` above `R_Xi>81/4`. The former polylogarithmic rate is retracted because its growing
   low-block Gram minimum was uncontrolled. The normalized-Hankel classification leaves maximal ground-edge collapse,
   nonvanishing edge overshoot, or near-minimal forced source alignment; isolated microlocal prime
   seams are now closed, so an interior survivor must be delocalized and coherently multi-seam.
   Macroscopic, unbounded, and bounded-away-from-zero negative schedules are classified out for a
   complete family; no further recession or ordinary finite-window analysis replaces the
   source-aligned growing-Gram small-singular-value problem.
   Only after admission, prove compactified even/odd occupancy convergence/every paired smoothing
   trace for a source-selected adjacent/interior branch.  One trace moment and regular
   vector/resolvent convergence are insufficient.
9. **D2:** obtain a literal high-frequency ceiling for the continued prime--Gamma source. Route N
   is a terminalized receiver, not a parallel live task.
10. **P/Q:** construct P's compact positive carrier or prove Q's direct Cassini--Jensen/jet
    saturation. Q's selected continuous power-reciprocal carrier is retired; any reuse requires a
    source-derived discontinuous plateau quotient rather than all-degree scalar closure.

### Work unlikely to move frontier

- more RH-equivalent scalar coordinates;
- finite-prefix positivity;
- generic compactness without source admission;
- more Route O seed asymptotics without ground-space classification;
- more Route E moments or seam patch algebra without unit physical incidence;
- more Route-E/GHS mean-value, fibre-kernel, or maximal-iteration packaging without either the
  published averaged-Chowla premise, `OffDiagonalSmall mu hh`, or rate-sharp physical
  weighted-projection/all-reporter law;
- more fixed-height/PNT-Abel or rough-number estimates for Route E without entering the remaining
  prime off-diagonal Ramaré/Halász range;
- more Route R frames or orientation detectors without independent singular source annihilation;
- more Route U positive carriers, cumulant signs, fourth-cumulant reserve restatements, or sector
  bounds without precompression adaptive source orientation;
- builds or formalization of conjectures whose mathematical source theorem is not proved.

### Route S and cross-cutting arithmetic source infrastructure

`ClosedHyperplaneInverse.lean`, `CompactHyperplanePerturbation.lean`,
`PolynomialCircuitParametrisation.lean`, and `CircuitExceptionalTimes.lean` are umbrella-reachable
at this cutoff. `ModulusBudget`, `ModulusDivergence`, `AdaptiveEigenframeTwoQuerySourceMemory`,
`CoeffDenominatorBound`, the complete Eisenstein chain,
`CriticalSchurIntegralClosurePlace`, the primitive-execution chain, the defect ladder, vacuum
bound, Newton--Puiseux theorem, graded-genesis capstone, symbolic-execution gap, and the other
anchors identified as wired below receive ordinary formal credit.
`CofinalInverseCapacity.lean`, its Hilbert and weighted-port descendants, and
`TorusInvariantField.lean` are umbrella-reachable at this cutoff.

### Frankenstein seam: source-horizontal neutral jet = Route-T residual increment

Umbrella-reachable `SourceHorizontalWoldNeutralJetBridge.lean` proves a new coefficient-faithful
cross-route identity.
The neutral derivative of the signed source Gamma Bockstein, through a uniformly invertible
primewise gauge in `[1/16,9/8]`, is exactly the depth-one Wold coefficient.  Subtracting the
relative holonomy of consecutive prime labels supplies the previously unmatched ordered log-mesh
cocycle.  The resulting reflection-odd deformation has neutral jet `R_{N+1}-R_N`; after composing
with each `XiZeroCopy` centered rate it preserves divisor multiplicity and literal reflection
orientation.

The neutral-jet theorem alone does not prove RH: `R_N -> 0` controls only neutral jets.  The
prime-clock completion below now supplies the formerly missing nonzero-grade cofinal law.  It is
one literal source object spanning Routes R and T, rather than two analogous coefficient packages.

### Prime-clock nonlinear polar properness [FORMAL — NOT A NEW ROUTE]

The source-horizontal primitive itself has a new asymptotic candidate beyond its neutral jet.
After scaling horizontal grade by the terminal clock `log p_N`, its linear derivative is
`R_N/log p_N -> 0`, while the completed finite quadrature gives normalized cubic coefficient
`-2/3`. This universal odd cubic remnant proves that neutral-jet collapse does not make the full
source deformation trivial. Umbrella-reachable
`SourceHorizontalWoldPrimeClockCubic.lean` certifies the natural-cutoff Wold coefficient `1/3`,
quantitative Abel error, complete prime-clock `-2/3` limit, Xi-copy cubic value, reflection
oddness, zero iff the copy is critical, and the literal first/third neutral-jet realization.
`SourceHorizontalWoldPrimeClockProfile.lean` completes all orders:
normalized mass and moments converge to `1/(r+1)`, order-uniform domination permits dominated
summation, exact Taylor resummation recovers the finite coefficient-faithful source deformation,
and its prime-clock limit is the universal odd profile

```text
F(u)=sum_(k>=0) u^(2k+1)/(2k+1)! * (1/(2k+1)-1).
```

It proves `F(u)=0` exactly at `u=0`. Pairing `F` with the complete-resolution tail gives the
reflection-even polar work `-(log 2/2)uF(u)`, positive exactly off line. More strongly,

```text
(log 2/18)u^4 <= -(log 2/2)uF(u),
```

so every nonzero grade has unbounded work under expanding source dilation. For every fixed `d>1`,
global bounded polar-orbit work over all multiplicity-labelled Xi copies is formally equivalent to
RH. This does not prove RH: bounded label-blind completion and signed codiagonal conservation do
not control the nonlinear cofinal observer. Route L's newly paper-closed critical-edge Köthe wall
is the reciprocal-domain shadow of the same properness principle: both orientations land exactly
at zero grade. The exact Frankenstein producer is now source-derived simultaneous
forward/reciprocal landing, or an equivalent pre-zero bound on this literal polar orbit. Ambient
`l1/l2` admission, reflection exchange, and positivity do not supply that theorem.

## Final frontier statement

Corpus has constructed source, completed landing, many positive geometries, and exact RH
receivers. It also proves why bounded label-blind completion, generic positivity, local exactness,
finite moments, isometry, and determinant packaging erase or fail to orient RH-bearing
coordinate.

Current frontier consists of eleven genuinely different lettered source problems plus the
unlettered CCM-FU incidence problem, concentrated in singular descent, oscillatory all-prime
transport, adaptive boundary orientation, whole-space coercivity, and positive carrier existence.
Routes T and U are real additions. Source-horizontal,
Burnol, eigen-pair, centered-prime-tail, Gamma--Lorentz, and universal-law-field additions narrow
existing routes or strengthen receiver/no-go infrastructure; they do not add another lettered
route. The Euler q-Gamma/abelian-gas process is a genuine new pre-zero source-to-divisor
architecture, now
including formal global class-law, expectation, depth-divisor, KMS uniqueness, RG-dilation, and
natural-boundary calculus in addition to relative-entropy evaluation, positive GNS/C-star
realization and C-star-level state uniqueness, hypertranscendence, and zero-free genus-one erasure,
the complete modulus-defect ladder, wired modulus budget and prime-window nonsummability, and
nonsummable condensed-phase symmetrization,
but it lacks a critical-line orientation and RH handoff, so it remains a candidate rather than
Route V. Its marked parameter commutator, socle translation/holomorphy/hypertranscendence, and
complete socle-divisor order are formal; fugacity entropy and recurrence/open lift remain open. The
two-source exchange-versus-mixed-transgression noncollapse is formal, but also has no RH handoff.
Four additional Route-V candidates are now mathematically substantial.
The conservative Euler-shell/Witt completion has an exact formal receiver and a Lean
RH-equivalent pointwise selection gate but lacks the
literal jointly convergent source/exterior inverse pair; one common Banach receiver is formally
excluded. The triptic/elliptic-gamma branch has a complete orbital-divisor normal form and finite
realization theorem, now with exact additive and multiplicative kernel converses, but lacks a
coefficient-faithful Xi handoff and reflection orientation. Tropical-projective source-normal
descent has exact prime-clock projective width and recurrence, but lacks an Xi-typed observer and
descent theorem. Relative formation-completion has an exact umbrella-reachable centered-jet
quotient/evenization core, but lacks its literal arithmetic relative extension and
source-normal hidden-current annihilation law. None satisfies the corpus definition of an
instantiated route.

The Gauss-component residue hierarchy is an adjacent local Connes candidate whose actual circle
operators, principal factorization, finite iteration, full two-frequency diagonal, sandwich fold,
relative one-step bound, rank-one operator logarithmic residue, and `m>=2` ratio-weighted
translation estimate, general half-power tail closure, literal principal-step identity, and
operator-power telescope are umbrella-reachable, and the first propagated-iterate depth is
closed: the relative one-step and double-defect estimates hold in umbrella Lean for every
`m >= 2`, giving the operator logarithmic-mean residue law at `m = 2`. The label-faithful
multi-place assembly is also umbrella-reachable, with per-label residue laws for the actual
assembled operator at `m <= 2`, exact codiagonal collapse onto the paired scalar, an injective
cofinal canonical aperture, and a carrier-compatible positive residue current. Its Hodge mass
pairing is now nonnegative, reflection-even, and has exact critical radical; vanishing is RH.
The GNS socket and Krein decomposition now show exactly why local sign is insufficient: the Weil
form is unconditionally pair-sum energy minus reflection-difference energy, and eliminating the
negative sector is itself equivalent to RH and Hodge-mass vanishing. Local sign and indefinite
realization are therefore closed; direct reflection-pair square completion is terminal. The
missing theorem is an independent arithmetic global trace/intersection identity
forcing that positive mass to vanish, not another pairing construction. Deeper
propagated-iterate control (`m >= 3`),
identification with the actual Weil/intersection object, and that global annihilating trace remain.
Route L's cofinal prime-period tomography now proves algebraic label sufficiency and topological
non-strictness, so more compact-orbit periodization is not progress. Route T's lower-half residual
is smooth and absolutely connection-summable; its exact safe-side symbol, Wold meromorphic
continuation, critical-boundary higher-valuation holomorphy, critical and off-critical poles, and
prime-gap obstruction equivalence are formal. Absolute critical graph closure is now formally
retired: it suppresses unconditional critical Wold poles. Its scientific wall is signed or
distributional continuation/noncancellation of the complete Hardy--mesh--holonomy identity toward
the physical Laplace boundary, together with Maynard aggregate large-values control. Route U has strict canonical
Jacobi flux in every finite positive chamber, complete first-face classification, and formal
one-block capacity at every later positive even contact. Its paper-level parity-jet law now
classifies repeated-root radical flags and their odd inertia. Its wall is odd-sector/full-ground
admission at the selected even hard edge, with cofinal contact-order and face-degree escape
upstream of that boundary.

The coalitional/collective-repair/cofinal-viability laws, primitive-degree escape, and observer
execution-gap laws expose a second route-design primitive, but no arithmetic repair family or Xi
handoff exists. Exact Hedenmalm source spectrum, determinant/Riesz structure, essential
inverse-flow/Weil package, squared Cayley receiver, closed weighted derivative,
shifted-cokernel/source equation, and supersymmetric Dirac are now one umbrella-reachable C1
opening. The direct-Cayley/two-port extension is now umbrella-reachable and remains a receiver; it
does not define an independent route.
Shilov matrix/tower and the now-complete commutative C*-envelope/boundary-representation model,
cofinal Gamma relation law, congruence-symbol no-go, and constitutive repair laws strengthen
receiver and obstruction bookkeeping, not source production.
Nyman--Beurling finite sections likewise strengthen a classical receiver, while the generalized
Beurling system layer is a Route-E firewall rather than a new producer.

RH remains open. Any claim of progress must now prove one named source theorem above, refute one
route with an exact no-go and positive survivor, or construct a literal new source architecture
with an unconditional handoff not source-isogenous to an existing route.
